# Latin words without a link

Generated by `python3 scripts/latin_unlinked.py`; do not edit by hand. The builder links every word of a term’s head and of an example to the page of the word it is a form of. This is what it could not link, in the heads of terms of several words, the examples and the Latin quoted as evidence. The likely lemma is a guess from the headwords of Lewis & Short.

| | Words | Linked | Ambiguous | Without an entry | Names without an entry |
| --- | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: |
| Heads of several words | 9,075 | 7,149 | 749 | 1,103 | 74 |
| Examples | 67,955 | 58,105 | 5,921 | 3,224 | 705 |
| Quotations in evidence | 44,254 | 37,425 | 3,913 | 2,226 | 690 |
| **All** | 121,284 | 102,679 (84.7%) | 10,583 (8.7%) | 6,553 (5.4%) | 1,469 (1.2%) |

Latin quoted inside comments, registers and notes is not linked: 21,651 words, of which 17,412 would be linked, 1,900 are ambiguous and 2,339 have no entry.

Of the 6,553 words without an entry, 5,761 have none at all (3,433 likely lemmas), 111 are forms the index does not reach of a word that has one, and 538 are found only when their macrons are ignored. A capitalised word that opens a sentence and is a name elsewhere is counted with the names below.
